> AGENT

Structurally, it’s in decent shape for a young/small Python package. The layout is coherent: `workflow.py` is the IR core, `ingest/` converts external workflow shapes, `registry/` resolves templates, `runtime/` executes, `commands/` owns CLI entry points, and `analysis/search/schema/patches/blocks` are reasonably separated.

The main weakness is coupling around public facades and CLI helpers. `vibecomfy/__init__.py`, `registry/__init__.py`, and `runtime/__init__.py` re-export a lot, and a few command modules import helpers back from `vibecomfy.cli`, which makes the CLI layer a little more tangled than it needs to be. I’d eventually move shared workflow/path resolution out of `cli.py` into a neutral module.

**Scan Scores**
| Score | Value |
|---|---:|
| Overall | 20.0/100 |
| Strict | 20.0/100 |
| Objective/mechanical | 80.2/100 |
| Verified | 80.2/100 |

The harsh `20.0` is because 20 subjective dimensions are unreviewed and counted as zero. The useful mechanical read is `80.2/100`.

The next structural improvements should be: increase meaningful test coverage, untangle facade/import cycles, move shared CLI resolution helpers out of `cli.py`, and review the flagged security items. 2. **Move CLI shared helpers out of `cli.py`**
   - `_resolve_workflow_path` should live in something like `vibecomfy/commands/workflow_args.py` or `vibecomfy/registry/resolve.py`.
   - Then `analyze.py`, `convert.py`, and `inspect.py` stop importing back from the CLI entrypoint.

3. **Clean dependency metadata**

6. **Improve test health**

> AGENT

The broader phase change made objective work interrupt postflight assessment, and existing tests correctly reject that. I’m narrowing it: only explicitly queued review/concern findings should bypass the postflight assessment gate in plan mode; objective work keeps the existing postflight behavior.

> TOOL

tool_use apply_patch
```json
{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: /tmp/desloppify-fix-parent/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/snapshot.py\n@@\n-    # Suppress postflight signals (assessment/workflow/triage/review) when\n-    # execution work exists and we're either in execute mode or have no plan.\n-    # Without a plan, objective work always takes priority over postflight items.\n-    suppress_postflight_signals = has_execution and (\n-        raw_phase == \"execute\" or raw_phase is None\n-    )\n+    # Suppress postflight signals (assessment/workflow/triage/review) whenever\n+    # explicit execution work exists. Durable queue_order entries are the\n+    # user's executable plan; postflight reassessment prompts must not make\n+    # `next` report an empty or unrelated queue while planned work is live.\n+    suppress_postflight_signals = has_execution\n*** End Patch\n"
}
```

> TOOL

tool_use apply_patch
```json
{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: /tmp/desloppify-fix-parent/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/snapshot.py\n@@\n from desloppify.engine._state.issue_semantics import (\n     counts_toward_objective_backlog,\n     is_assessment_request,\n+    is_review_work_item,\n     is_triage_finding,\n )\n@@\n-    # Suppress postflight signals (assessment/workflow/triage/review) whenever\n-    # explicit execution work exists. Durable queue_order entries are the\n-    # user's executable plan; postflight reassessment prompts must not make\n-    # `next` report an empty or unrelated queue while planned work is live.\n-    suppress_postflight_signals = has_execution\n+    has_explicit_review_execution = any(\n+        is_review_work_item(item) for item in explicit_queue_items\n+    )\n+    # Suppress postflight signals (assessment/workflow/triage/review) when\n+    # execution work exists and we're in execute mode, have no plan, or the\n+    # durable plan explicitly queued imported review findings. Objective work\n+    # discovered during postflight remains backlog-only until postflight ends,\n+    # but review findings imported into queue_order must stay executable.\n+    suppress_postflight_signals = has_execution and (\n+        raw_phase == \"execute\" or raw_phase is None or has_explicit_review_execution\n+    )\n*** End Patch\n"
}

> AGENT

Yes, it was a real bug. I traced and validated a fix.

What was wrong:
- `.desloppify/plan.json` had `28` live open review IDs in `queue_order`.
- `desloppify next` built its queue from `build_queue_snapshot()`.
- `_merge_execution_candidates()` filtered queued review findings out unless they were also in an active cluster.
- Result: plan/status saw the work, but `next` saw no executable items.

There was a related progress-summary bug too:
- After fixing queue selection, `next` showed the right item but still called the 28 planned items “stale”.
- `_snapshot_item_ids()` did not count `execution_items`, only the older partition fields.

I could not write directly to `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify` because this sandbox only allows edits in the current `vibecomfy` workspace and temp dirs. So I copied Desloppify to `/tmp/desloppify-fix-parent/desloppify`, patched it there, and saved the patch here:

[desloppify-queue-review-fix.patch](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy/desloppify-queue-review-fix.patch)

Validation against the current `vibecomfy` Desloppify state, using the patched copy:

```text
desloppify next
Queue: 1 item
The block Protocol and @block decorator create a marker abstraction...
Queue: 28 items (28 planned)
```

```text
desloppify plan queue --top 5
Queue: 28 items (28 planned)
1 high   review  The block Protocol...
2 high   review  Public block functions...
...
```

Focused tests in the patched Desloppify copy:

```text
132 passed in 1.08s
```

To apply it in the real Desloppify repo:

```bash

  desloppify/tests/commands/test_queue_progress.py
```

> TOOL

tool_use apply_patch
```json
{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py\n@@\n from __future__ import annotations\n \n from dataclasses import dataclass, field\n+from inspect import signature\n from types import MappingProxyType\n from typing import Any, Callable, Mapping, Protocol, TypeVar\n \n from vibecomfy.workflow import VibeWorkflow\n@@\n class Block(Protocol):\n     def __call__(self, workflow: VibeWorkflow, **kwargs: Any) -> Handles:\n         \"\"\"Mutate the workflow as needed (any VibeWorkflow method) and return handles for downstream wiring.\"\"\"\n \n \n+@dataclass(frozen=True)\n+class BlockSpec:\n+    name: str\n+    module: str\n+    qualname: str\n+    signature: str\n+\n+\n BlockFn = TypeVar(\"BlockFn\", bound=Callable[..., Handles])\n \n+_BLOCK_REGISTRY: dict[str, Block] = {}\n+\n \n def block(fn: BlockFn) -> BlockFn:\n+    sig = signature(fn)\n+    first_param = next(iter(sig.parameters.values()), None)\n+    if first_param is None or first_param.name != \"workflow\":\n+        raise TypeError(f\"{fn.__module__}.{fn.__qualname__} must accept workflow as its first parameter\")\n+    name = f\"{fn.__module__}.{fn.__name__}\"\n+    spec = BlockSpec(\n+        name=name,\n+        module=fn.__module__,\n+        qualname=fn.__qualname__,\n+        signature=str(sig),\n+    )\n+    setattr(fn, \"__vibecomfy_block__\", spec)\n+    _BLOCK_REGISTRY[name] = fn\n     return fn\n \n \n-__all__ = [\"Block\", \"Handles\", \"block\"]\n+def block_spec(fn: Callable[..., Any]) -> BlockSpec | None:\n+    spec = getattr(fn, \"__vibecomfy_block__\", None)\n+    return spec if isinstance(spec, BlockSpec) else None\n+\n+\n+def registered_blocks() -> Mapping[str, Block]:\n+    return MappingProxyType(dict(_BLOCK_REGISTRY))\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\"Block\", \"BlockSpec\", \"Handles\", \"block\", \"block_spec\", \"registered_blocks\"]\n*** End Patch\n"
}
```

The current package import does three things at once: defines `Patch`, imports built-in patch modules, and registers them. _patch_ltx(api, image_to_video=True)\n+        return True\n+    if workflow_id == \"flux2_klein_9b_gguf_t2i\":\n+        _patch_gguf(api)\n+        return True\n+    return False\n+\n+\n+def _patch_ltx(api: dict[str, Any], *, image_to_video: bool) -> None:\n+    for node in api.values():\n+        if not isinstance(node, dict):\n+            continue\n+        inputs = node.get(\"inputs\", {})\n+        for key, value in list(inputs.items()):\n+            if value == \"ltx-2.3-22b-dev.safetensors\":\n+                inputs[key] = LTX_CHECKPOINT\n+            elif value == \"comfy_gemma_3_12B_it.safetensors\":\n+                inputs[key] = LTX_TEXT_ENCODER\n+    api[\"3059\"][\"inputs\"].update({\"widget_0\": 384, \"widget_1\": 256, \"widget_2\": 9})\n+    api[\"4979\"][\"inputs\"][\"widget_0\"] = 9\n+    api[\"4978\"][\"inputs\"][\"widget_0\"] = 8\n+    api[\"1241\"][\"inputs\"][\"widget_0\"] = 8\n+    api[\"3980\"][\"inputs\"].update({\"widget_0\": 9, \"widget_1\": 8})\n+    api[\"4977\"][\"inputs\"][\"widget_0\"] = not image_to_video\n+    api[\"2004\"][\"inputs\"][\"widget_0\"] = \"egyptian_queen.png\" if image_to_video else \"example.png\"\n+    api[\"4981\"][\"inputs\"][\"widget_1\"] = 384\n+    api[\"4010\"][\"class_type\"] = \"LowVRAMAudioVAELoader\"\n+    api[\"4010\"][\"inputs\"] = {\"ckpt_name\": LTX_CHECKPOINT}\n+    api[\"3940\"][\"class_type\"] = \"LowVRAMCheckpointLoader\"\n+    api[\"3940\"][\"inputs\"] = {\"ckpt_name\": LTX_CHECKPOINT, \"dependencies\": [\"4960\", 0]}\n+\n+\n+def _patch_gguf(api: dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n+    for node in api.values():\n+        if not isinstance(node, dict):\n+            continue\n+        inputs = node.get(\"inputs\", {})\n+        if node.get(\"class_type\") == \"UNETLoader\" and \"unet_name\" in inputs:\n+            node[\"class_type\"] = \"UnetLoaderGGUF\"\n+            inputs[\"unet_name\"] = GGUF_MODEL\n+        if node.get(\"class_type\") == \"VAELoader\" and inputs.get(\"vae_name\") == \"full_encoder_small_decoder.safetensors\":\n+            inputs[\"vae_name\"] = FLUX_VAE\n+\n+\n+def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n+    subparsers = parser.add_subparsers(dest=\"cmd\", required=True)\n+    prepare = subparsers.add_parser(\"prepare-workflow\")\n+    prepare.add_argument(\"workflow_id\")\n+    prepare.add_argument(\"source\", type=Path)\n+    prepare.add_argument(\"output\", type=Path)\n+    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\n+\n+    if args.cmd == \"prepare-workflow\":\n+        print(prepare_workflow(args.workflow_id, args.source, args.output).as_posix())\n+        return 0\n+    raise AssertionError(args.cmd)\n+\n+\n+if __name__ == \"__main__\":\n+    raise SystemExit(main())\n*** End Patch\n"
}

\"STRING\"},\n+                        \"optional\": {\"mode\": [[\"fast\", \"slow\"], {\"default\": \"fast\"}]},\n+                    },\n+                    \"outputs\": [{\"type\": \"CONDITIONING\", \"name\": \"conditioning\"}],\n+                }\n+            ]\n+        ),\n+        encoding=\"utf-8\",\n+    )\n+\n+    schema = LocalSchemaProvider(index).get_schema(\"PromptNode\")\n+\n+    assert schema is not None\n+    assert schema.pack == \"core\"\n+    assert schema.inputs[\"text\"].type == \"STRING\"\n+    assert schema.inputs[\"text\"].required is True\n+    assert schema.inputs[\"mode\"].type == \"CHOICE\"\n+    assert schema.inputs[\"mode\"].choices == [\"fast\", \"slow\"]\n+    assert schema.inputs[\"mode\"].default == \"fast\"\n+    assert schema.outputs == [OutputSpec(type=\"CONDITIONING\", name=\"conditioning\")]\n+\n+\n+def test_malformed_object_info_cache_is_ignored(tmp_path) -> None:\n+    cache = tmp_path / \"object_info.bad.json\"\n+    cache.write_text(\"{not-json\", encoding=\"utf-8\")\n+\n+    assert load_object_info_cache(cache) is None\n+\n+\n+def test_runtime_schema_provider_reads_cached_object_info(tmp_path) -> None:\n+    provider = RuntimeSchemaProvider(server_url=\"http://runtime.test\", cache_dir=tmp_path)\n+    provider.cache_path.write_text(\n+        json.dumps(\n+            {\n+                \"RuntimeNode\": {\n+                    \"pack\": \"runtime-pack\",\n+                    \"input\": {\"required\": {\"image\": [\"IMAGE\", {\"default\": None}]}},\n+                    \"output\": [\"LATENT\"],\n+                    \"output_name\": [\"latent\"],\n+                }\n+            }\n+        ),\n+        encoding=\"utf-8\",\n+    )\n+\n+    schema = provider.get_schema(\"RuntimeNode\")\n+\n+    assert schema is not None\n+    assert schema.pack == \"runtime-pack\"\n+    assert schema.inputs[\"image\"].type == \"IMAGE\"\n+    assert schema.inputs[\"image\"].required is True\n+    assert schema.outputs == [OutputSpec(type=\"LATENT\", name=\"latent\")]\n+\n+\n+def test_runtime_schema_provider_fetches_and_writes_object_info_cache(tmp_path, monkeypatch) -> None:\n+    class FakeServer:\n+        async def __aenter__(self):\n+            return \"http://active-runtime.test\"\n+\n+        async def __aexit__(self, exc_type, exc, tb):\n+            return False\n+\n+    class FakeClient:\n+        def __init__(self, url: str) -> None:\n+            self.url = url\n+\n+        async def object_info(self):\n+            assert self.url == \"http://active-runtime.test\"\n+            return {\"FetchedNode\": {\"input\": {\"optional\": {\"strength\": [\"FLOAT\", {\"default\": 1.0, \"min\": 0, \"max\": 2}]}}}}\n+\n+    monkeypatch.setattr(\"vibecomfy.schema.provider.comfy_server\", lambda **kwargs: FakeServer())\n+    monkeypatch.setattr(\"vibecomfy.schema.provider.ComfyClient\", FakeClient)\n+    provider = RuntimeSchemaProvider(server_url=\"http://runtime.test\", cache_dir=tmp_path)\n+\n+    data = asyncio.run(provider.object_info_async())\n+\n+    assert \"FetchedNode\" in data\n+    assert json.loads(provider.cache_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")) == data\n+    schema = provider.get_schema(\"FetchedNode\")\n+    assert schema is not None\n+    assert schema.inputs[\"strength\"].default == 1.0\n+    assert schema.inputs[\"strength\"].min == 0\n+    assert schema.inputs[\"strength\"].max == 2\n+\n+\n+def test_get_schema_provider_auto_selection(tmp_path, monkeypatch) -> None:\n+    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)\n+    monkeypatch.setattr(\"vibecomfy.schema.provider.shutil.which\", lambda _: None)\n+\n+    assert isinstance(get_schema_provider(\"auto\"), LocalSchemaProvider)\n+    assert isinstance(get_schema_provider(\"auto\", server_url=\"http://runtime.test\"), RuntimeSchemaProvider)\n+\n+    (tmp_path / \"node_index.json\").write_text(\"[]\", encoding=\"utf-8\")\n+    assert isinstance(get_schema_provider(\"auto\"), LocalSchemaProvider)\n*** End Patch\n"
